<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.timesofisrael.com/gamblers-trying-to-win-a-bet-on-polymarket-are-vowing-to-kill-me-if-i-dont-rewrite-an-iran-missile-story/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Polymarket gamblers threaten to kill me over Iran missile story</span><span class='zh'>Polymarket 赌徒因伊朗导弹报道威胁要杀我</span></a><span class="dom">timesofisrael.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Reporters reveal threats from Polymarket gamblers over an Iran missile story.</span><span class='zh'>记者报道，有人在 Polymarket 对其伊朗导弹报道下注后威胁要杀害他人。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Reporters reveal threats from Polymarket gamblers over an Iran missile story. The threats underscore the real-world risks journalists face when covering volatile bets and geopolitical topics in real-time markets, and raise questions about safety and editorial resilience.</span><span class='zh'>记者报道，有人在 Polymarket 对其伊朗导弹报道下注后威胁要杀害他人。此事凸显在实时博彩市场中报道敏感国际议题时，记者面临的现实风险，以及安全与编辑独立性可能受到的挑战。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Astral will join OpenAI, signaling a consolidation of AI tooling and developer platforms. The move could accelerate integration of Astral&#x27;s capabilities into OpenAI&#x27;s ecosystem, influencing how developers build atop AI services.</span><span class='zh'>Astral 将加入 OpenAI，标志着 AI 工具链和开发者平台的整合。这一举措可能加速将 Astral 的能力融入 OpenAI 的生态，影响开发者在 AI 服务上的搭建方式。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A 12-year-old Palestinian boy describes how Israeli forces killed his family in a car, offering a rare child’s eyewitness account amid ongoing conflict.</span><span class='zh'>一名12岁巴勒斯坦男孩讲述在一起车内事件中，他的家人被以色列军队杀害的经过。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A 12-year-old Palestinian boy describes how Israeli forces killed his family in a car, offering a rare child’s eyewitness account amid ongoing conflict. The testimony highlights civilian harm and the human cost of military operations, underscoring the volatility of frontline reporting.</span><span class='zh'>一名12岁巴勒斯坦男孩讲述在一起车内事件中，他的家人被以色列军队杀害的经过。此证词突显冲突中的平民伤亡与人道代价，也反映出前线报道的挑战。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://opencode.ai/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://opencode.ai/social-share.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='opencode.ai' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">05</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://opencode.ai/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenCode – Open source AI coding agent</span><span class='zh'>OpenCode：开源AI编码代理</span></a><span class="dom">opencode.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenCode is an open-source AI coding agent that helps developers automate routine coding tasks and explore AI-assisted workflows.</span><span class='zh'>OpenCode 是一个开源的 AI 编码代理，旨在帮助开发者自动化常规编码任务并尝试 AI 驱动的工作流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenCode is an open-source AI coding agent that helps developers automate routine coding tasks and explore AI-assisted workflows. By being open-source, it invites experimentation, customization, and transparency, contrasting with proprietary copilots. This matters for developers weighing privacy, control, and ecosystem when adopting AI coding tools.</span><span class='zh'>OpenCode 是一个开源的 AI 编码代理，旨在帮助开发者自动化常规编码任务并尝试 AI 驱动的工作流。作为开源项目，它鼓励社区参与、定制和透明度，与闭源的代码助手形成对比。对于考虑隐私、控制权和生态系统的开发者来说，这是选择 AI 编码工具时一个重要考量。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">06</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://nypost.com/2026/03/18/us-news/afroman-found-not-liable-in-bizarre-ohio-defamation-case/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Afroman found not liable in defamation case</span><span class='zh'>Afroman 在诽谤案中被裁定对警察无责</span></a><span class="dom">nypost.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Afroman was found not liable in a defamation suit brought by Ohio police who had raided his home.</span><span class='zh'>Afroman 在俄亥俄州警察就突袭其家中提起的诽谤诉讼中被判无责任。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Afroman was found not liable in a defamation suit brought by Ohio police who had raided his home. The ruling emphasizes the high standard for proving defamation when public figures discuss police conduct and may influence future entertainment-law disputes. This outcome matters for creators who voice controversial statements.</span><span class='zh'>Afroman 在俄亥俄州警察就突袭其家中提起的诽谤诉讼中被判无责任。判决强调涉及公共人物与执法行为的言论案件需满足较高的证明标准，可能影响未来相关诉讼的走向，并对娱乐行业有一定参考意义。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://arstechnica.com/gadgets/2026/03/google-details-new-24-hour-process-to-sideload-unverified-android-apps/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/developer-identity-hero-1152x648.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='arstechnica.com' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">07</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arstechnica.com/gadgets/2026/03/google-details-new-24-hour-process-to-sideload-unverified-android-apps/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google details new 24-hour process to sideload unverified Android apps</span><span class='zh'>谷歌公布 24 小时内可 sideload 未验证 Android 应用的新流程</span></a><span class="dom">arstechnica.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google detailed a 24-hour window that allows sideloading of unverified Android apps to accelerate testing and internal distribution outside the Play ecosystem.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌公布了一项 24 小时窗口，允许 sideload 未经验证的 Android 应用以加速测试和内部分发。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.dyne.org/child-protection-is-not-access-control/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Do Not Turn Child Protection into Internet Access Control</span><span class='zh'>不要把儿童保护变成互联网访问控制</span></a><span class="dom">news.dyne.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Guardrails for child protection should be precise and targeted, not a pretext for blanket internet access control.</span><span class='zh'>儿童保护政策不应成为全面的网络访问控制的借口。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Guardrails for child protection should be precise and targeted, not a pretext for blanket internet access control. Overly broad censorship risks chilling freedom online while failing to stop abuse. The piece calls for privacy-preserving, surgical safeguards instead.</span><span class='zh'>儿童保护政策不应成为全面的网络访问控制的借口。宽泛的封锁容易压制在线自由，并未有效阻止滥用。应采用更精准、透明的隐私保护措施与安全防护。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.pew.org/en/research-and-analysis/articles/2026/03/18/austins-surge-of-new-housing-construction-drove-down-rents" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Austin’s surge of new housing construction drove down rents</span><span class='zh'>奥斯汀新建住房激增致租金下降</span></a><span class="dom">pew.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Austin’s housing boom kept rents in check as a wave of new units came online, softening affordability even as demand from tech workers remains high.</span><span class='zh'>奥斯汀的住房供给激增带动新建单位上线，抵消了需求，导致租金走低。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Austin’s housing boom kept rents in check as a wave of new units came online, softening affordability even as demand from tech workers remains high. The finding highlights how supplying more units can influence urban affordability and inform policy debates on zoning and infrastructure.</span><span class='zh'>奥斯汀的住房供给激增带动新建单位上线，抵消了需求，导致租金走低。此现象反映出供给侧扩张对城市可负担性的直接影响，可能影响土地使用、基础设施和住房政策的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://thatshubham.com/blog/news-audit" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The 49MB web page</span><span class='zh'>49MB 的网页</span></a><span class="dom">thatshubham.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The page weighs in at 49MB, serving as a cautionary example of how modern sites can balloon with images, scripts, and third-party resources.</span><span class='zh'>该网页达到 49MB，揭示现代站点如何被图片、脚本和第三方资源膨胀。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The page weighs in at 49MB, serving as a cautionary example of how modern sites can balloon with images, scripts, and third-party resources. The write-up walks through auditing approaches to identify bloated bundles, optimize critical paths, and pare back assets without wrecking UX. Takeaway: page size is a first-class performance lever, and thoughtful auditing matters for both users and developers.</span><span class='zh'>该网页达到 49MB，揭示现代站点如何被图片、脚本和第三方资源膨胀。文中提供审计方法，帮助识别冗余资源、优化关键渲染路径，并在不破坏用户体验的前提下精简资产。要点：页面大小是性能的重要变量，细致的审计对用户和开发者都重要。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.tomshardware.com/video-games/console-gaming/microsofts-unhackable-xbox-one-has-been-hacked-by-bliss-the-2013-console-finally-fell-to-voltage-glitching-allowing-the-loading-of-unsigned-code-at-every-level" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Microsoft&#x27;s &#x27;unhackable&#x27; Xbox One has been hacked by &#x27;Bliss&#x27;</span><span class='zh'>微软所谓的“不可破解”的 Xbox One 被 Bliss 破解</span></a><span class="dom">tomshardware.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers demonstrated a voltage-glitch attack against Microsoft&#x27;s Xbox One, breaking the console&#x27;s claimed &#x27;unhackable&#x27; security.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员通过电压干扰对 Xbox One 展开攻击，打破了其“不可破解”的安全宣称。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers demonstrated a voltage-glitch attack against Microsoft&#x27;s Xbox One, breaking the console&#x27;s claimed &#x27;unhackable&#x27; security. The hack lets unsigned code run at various privilege levels, undermining hardware protections and enabling homebrew or piracy-style access.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员通过电压干扰对 Xbox One 展开攻击，打破了其“不可破解”的安全宣称。该漏洞使未签名代码在不同权限级别下得以运行，削弱了硬件防护，并可能开启自制软件或盗版访问。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.science.org/content/article/arxiv-pioneering-preprint-server-declares-independence-cornell" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ArXiv declares independence from Cornell</span><span class='zh'>ArXiv宣布独立于康奈尔大学</span></a><span class="dom">science.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ArXiv, the long-running open-access preprint server, is shedding its longtime host by shifting governance away from Cornell.</span><span class='zh'>作为长期负责的开放获取预印本服务器，ArXiv 正在将治理从康奈尔大学转向独立管理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ArXiv, the long-running open-access preprint server, is shedding its longtime host by shifting governance away from Cornell. The move signals a push toward community-led stewardship and diversified funding as arXiv seeks long-term sustainability. For researchers, it could mean changes in governance, policy, and how quickly new features roll out.</span><span class='zh'>作为长期负责的开放获取预印本服务器，ArXiv 正在将治理从康奈尔大学转向独立管理。此次转变旨在实现更具社区驱动色彩的治理结构和多元化资金来源，以确保长期可持续性。对于研究者而言，未来在治理、政策和新功能上线速度等方面可能出现变化。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mistral.ai/news/leanstral" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Leanstral: Open-source agent for trustworthy coding and formal proof engineering</span><span class='zh'>Leanstral：用于可信编码与形式化证明工程的开源代理</span></a><span class="dom">mistral.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Leanstral is an open-source agent designed to assist developers with trustworthy coding and formal proof engineering.</span><span class='zh'>Leanstral 是一个面向开发者的开源代理，旨在协助进行可信编码与形式化证明工程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Leanstral is an open-source agent designed to assist developers with trustworthy coding and formal proof engineering. It integrates AI-assisted coding with formal verification tooling to improve software correctness and reliability, making rigorous development workflows more accessible.</span><span class='zh'>Leanstral 是一个面向开发者的开源代理，旨在协助进行可信编码与形式化证明工程。它将 AI 辅助编码与形式化验证工具结合，提升软件正确性与可信度，让严格的软件工程工作流更易落地。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Delve dives into the emerging &#x27;Fake Compliance as a Service&#x27; phenomenon, critiquing services that generate pretend attestations or simplified controls to appease audits.</span><span class='zh'>Delve 深度剖析新兴的“假合规即服务”现象，批评那些提供虚假合规证明或简化控制的服务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Delve dives into the emerging &#x27;Fake Compliance as a Service&#x27; phenomenon, critiquing services that generate pretend attestations or simplified controls to appease audits. The piece warns about integrity risks for users, regulators, and auditors when compliance is commodified.</span><span class='zh'>Delve 深度剖析新兴的“假合规即服务”现象，批评那些提供虚假合规证明或简化控制的服务。文章警示当合规被商品化时，对用户、监管机构和审计方构成的信任与合规性风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.reuters.com/business/finance/us-sec-preparing-eliminate-quarterly-reporting-requirement-wsj-says-2026-03-16/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>US SEC preparing to scrap quarterly reporting requirement</span><span class='zh'>美国SEC拟取消季度报告要求</span></a><span class="dom">reuters.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The U.S. Securities and Exchange Commission is preparing to eliminate the mandatory quarterly reporting requirement for public companies.</span><span class='zh'>美国证监会正筹划取消强制性季度财报，可能减轻上市公司合规负担，降低季度业绩波动。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The U.S. Securities and Exchange Commission is preparing to eliminate the mandatory quarterly reporting requirement for public companies. This could cut compliance costs and reduce quarterly volatility, but may shift investor expectations toward annual disclosures and alter how markets price near-term risk.</span><span class='zh'>美国证监会正筹划取消强制性季度财报，可能减轻上市公司合规负担，降低季度业绩波动。此举或改变投资者对时点披露的期待，并影响市场对短期风险的定价和分析方法。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.frontiersin.org/journals/political-science/articles/10.3389/fpos.2026.1779810/full" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Corruption erodes social trust more in democracies than in autocracies</span><span class='zh'>腐败在民主国家对社会信任的侵蚀比专制国家更严重</span></a><span class="dom">frontiersin.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A new study finds corruption erodes social trust more in democratic countries than in autocracies.</span><span class='zh'>最新研究显示，腐败对社会信任的侵蚀在民主国家比在专制国家更明显。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A new study finds corruption erodes social trust more in democratic countries than in autocracies. The result suggests that transparency and accountability mechanisms in democracies can backfire when corruption spreads, hurting trust in institutions more than in tightly controlled regimes. The finding matters for governance, civic tech, and how platforms rely on public trust.</span><span class='zh'>最新研究显示，腐败对社会信任的侵蚀在民主国家比在专制国家更明显。这一发现提示，民主制度的透明与问责机制在某些情境下可能放大信任的下滑，对治理、公民科技以及依赖信任的平台都具重要影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PC Gamer curates a selection of RSS readers in a long, 37MB feature. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://openrocket.info/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenRocket</span><span class='zh'>OpenRocket：开源火箭设计与仿真工具</span></a><span class="dom">openrocket.info</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenRocket is an open-source rocket design and flight simulation package that lets hobbyists, students, and researchers model aerodynamics, stability, and trajectories before launch.</span><span class='zh'>OpenRocket 是一个开源的火箭设计与飞行仿真工具，用户能在发射前建模气动、稳定性和轨迹。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenRocket is an open-source rocket design and flight simulation package that lets hobbyists, students, and researchers model aerodynamics, stability, and trajectories before launch. It helps verify designs and teaches rocketry principles without expensive hardware. The project embodies the practical value of open scientific tooling.</span><span class='zh'>OpenRocket 是一个开源的火箭设计与飞行仿真工具，用户能在发射前建模气动、稳定性和轨迹。它便于爱好者、学生和研究者在没有昂贵设备的情况下验证设计、学习火箭学原理。该项目体现了开源科学工具的实用价值。</span></p>

Takeaway: as AI-generated content proliferates, tools and norms for quality matter more than ever.</span><span class='zh'>Stop Sloppypasta 致力于提升 AI 生成内容的质量与负责任的使用，提供更好的提示设计、署名与质量检查的指南与工具。要点：随着 AI 内容的泛滥，质量标准与工具变得比以往更为重要。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Le Monde investigation shows real-time tracking of France&#x27;s aircraft carrier via fitness app data, revealing how public location sharing can leak sensitive security information.</span><span class='zh'>勒蒙德的调查显示，公开的健身应用数据可能让法国航母的位置在实时暴露，暴露了公共数据对国家安全的潜在威胁。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Le Monde investigation shows real-time tracking of France&#x27;s aircraft carrier via fitness app data, revealing how public location sharing can leak sensitive security information.</span><span class='zh'>勒蒙德的调查显示，公开的健身应用数据可能让法国航母的位置在实时暴露，暴露了公共数据对国家安全的潜在威胁。此事凸显了个人位置信息在缺乏隐私保护的情况下对安全的影响，促使对数据共享进行审视。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://haskellforall.com/2026/03/a-sufficiently-detailed-spec-is-code" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://haskellforall.com/2026/03/a-sufficiently-detailed-spec-is-code/opengraph-image?133960dfd41055ac' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='haskellforall.com' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">34</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://haskellforall.com/2026/03/a-sufficiently-detailed-spec-is-code" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A sufficiently detailed spec is code</span><span class='zh'>足够详细的规格就是代码</span></a><span class="dom">haskellforall.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Detailed specifications can serve as concrete, testable artifacts that drive implementation without separate code.</span><span class='zh'>足够详尽的规格本身就能成为可执行的产物，推动实现、测试与仿真，模糊了文档与代码的界线。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Detailed specifications can serve as concrete, testable artifacts that drive implementation without separate code. When specs are precise, teams can simulate behavior and verify outcomes directly from the docs.</span><span class='zh'>足够详尽的规格本身就能成为可执行的产物，推动实现、测试与仿真，模糊了文档与代码的界线。把规范当作代码来管理，可以用自动化校验与模拟来验证行为。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>LLM Architecture Gallery presents a visual catalog of common and emerging LLM architectures, from vanilla transformer stacks to retrieval-augmented and modular designs.</span><span class='zh'>LLM 架构画廊以可视化形式整理了常见与新兴的模型架构，从基础的 Transformer 堆栈到检索增强和模块化设计，帮助工程师对比在延迟、内存与精度等维度的权衡。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>LLM Architecture Gallery presents a visual catalog of common and emerging LLM architectures, from vanilla transformer stacks to retrieval-augmented and modular designs. It helps engineers compare trade-offs in latency, memory, and accuracy. Takeaway: architecture choices shape capabilities and cost, and a visual guide helps map the design space.</span><span class='zh'>LLM 架构画廊以可视化形式整理了常见与新兴的模型架构，从基础的 Transformer 堆栈到检索增强和模块化设计，帮助工程师对比在延迟、内存与精度等维度的权衡。要点：架构选择决定能力和成本，直观的对比有助于把握设计空间。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://bramcohen.com/p/manyana" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The future of version control</span><span class='zh'>版本控制的未来</span></a><span class="dom">bramcohen.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Visionaries describe the next phase of version control beyond Git-style systems, spotlighting peer-to-peer collaboration, cryptographic integrity, and scalable, offline-friendly workflows.</span><span class='zh'>讨论了超越现有版本控制系统的新范式，强调点对点协作、加密完整性和可离线扩展的工作流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Visionaries describe the next phase of version control beyond Git-style systems, spotlighting peer-to-peer collaboration, cryptographic integrity, and scalable, offline-friendly workflows. The takeaway is that new paradigms could change how teams coordinate, verify history, and scale development. For technologists, this signals a shift in toolchains and trust conventions.</span><span class='zh'>讨论了超越现有版本控制系统的新范式，强调点对点协作、加密完整性和可离线扩展的工作流。核心在于新机制可能改变团队的协作、历史验证和开发扩展的方式。对开发者来说，这预示着工具链与信任体系的变革。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://daringfireball.net/2026/03/your_frustration_is_the_product" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>“Your frustration is the product”</span><span class='zh'>『你的挫折就是产品』</span></a><span class="dom">daringfireball.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that many products monetize user frustration through friction and attention traps.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为许多产品通过制造挫折感、设计摩擦和注意力陷阱来变现。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that many products monetize user frustration through friction and attention traps. It urges designers to rethink engagement strategies and prioritize user autonomy and transparency.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为许多产品通过制造挫折感、设计摩擦和注意力陷阱来变现。呼吁设计师重新审视参与策略，优先考虑用户自主性与透明度。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://apenwarr.ca/log/20260316" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Every layer of review makes you 10x slower</span><span class='zh'>每多一层评审，速度就会慢十倍</span></a><span class="dom">apenwarr.ca</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An argument that added review layers multiply latency, urging leaner processes.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为每增加一层评审都会让交付速度慢十倍，评审层级的叠加带来显著延迟。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An argument that added review layers multiply latency, urging leaner processes. The post argues for reducing handoffs and embracing light-weight reviews, automation, and clear ownership to keep velocity high.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为每增加一层评审都会让交付速度慢十倍，评审层级的叠加带来显著延迟。文章主张减少不必要的环节，加强自动化和明确的职责分工，以维持高开发节奏。</span></p>

This showcases the potential for on-device, offline text-to-speech on resource-constrained hardware.</span><span class='zh'>展示的三个 Kitten TTS 模型体积极小，最小版本不到 25MB，凸显在边缘设备上实现高质量文本转语音的可行性。小体积模型有助于离线应用、低带宽环境，以及快速本地化部署。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/03/blocking-internet-archive-wont-stop-ai-it-will-erase-webs-historical-record" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Blocking Internet Archive Won&#x27;t Stop AI, but Will Erase Web&#x27;s Historical Record</span><span class='zh'>阻断 Internet Archive 不会阻止 AI，但会抹去网络的历史记录</span></a><span class="dom">eff.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Blocking Internet Archive won&#x27;t curb AI development, and models will still train on data from other sources.</span><span class='zh'>阻断 Internet Archive 不会遏制 AI 发展，模型仍会从其他来源获取数据。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Blocking Internet Archive won&#x27;t curb AI development, and models will still train on data from other sources. It would erase a vital historical snapshot of the web, harming researchers, journalists, and accountability. Smarter data-use policies are preferable to outright blackouts.</span><span class='zh'>阻断 Internet Archive 不会遏制 AI 发展，模型仍会从其他来源获取数据。这会抹去网络的宝贵历史快照，损害研究、报道和问责能力。应采用更聪明的数据使用政策，而不是全面封锁。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://engineering.fb.com/2026/03/02/data-infrastructure/investing-in-infrastructure-metas-renewed-commitment-to-jemalloc/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Meta’s renewed commitment to jemalloc</span><span class='zh'>Meta 再次承诺优化 jemalloc</span></a><span class="dom">engineering.fb.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Meta renews its commitment to jemalloc, the memory allocator powering its data infrastructure.</span><span class='zh'>Meta 再次承诺继续优化 jemalloc，这一为数据基础设施提供分配内存的组件在大规模系统中扮演关键角色。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Meta renews its commitment to jemalloc, the memory allocator powering its data infrastructure. The move signals continued focus on performance, latency, and memory efficiency at scale, with potential upstream contributions benefiting other projects that rely on jemalloc.</span><span class='zh'>Meta 再次承诺继续优化 jemalloc，这一为数据基础设施提供分配内存的组件在大规模系统中扮演关键角色。此举表明 Meta 将持续提升性能与内存效率，并可能向 jemalloc 的开源社区带来贡献，惠及依赖该分配器的其他项目。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Karpathy&#x27;s US Job Market Visualizer maps demand, salaries, and geographic distribution across tech sectors, offering a data-driven view of where skills matter. It&#x27;s a useful tool for engineers plotting career moves in AI and software.</span><span class='zh'>Karpathy 的美国就业市场可视化工具绘制了科技领域的需求、薪资和地理分布，为判断 AI 与软件技能的市场前景提供数据化视角。对职业规划很有参考价值。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://fidget-spinner.github.io/posts/jit-on-track.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Python 3.15&#x27;s JIT is now back on track</span><span class='zh'>Python 3.15 的 JIT 已重新回到正轨</span></a><span class="dom">fidget-spinner.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Python 3.15&#x27;s JIT development has resumed, signaling renewed momentum for speeding up Python without breaking existing C-extensions.</span><span class='zh'>Python 3.15 的 JIT 开发已重新启动，意味着在不破坏现有扩展的前提下提升 Python 运行速度的努力又有了新动能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Python 3.15&#x27;s JIT development has resumed, signaling renewed momentum for speeding up Python without breaking existing C-extensions. It aims to standardize prompts, enforce workflows, and improve reproducibility across teams.</span><span class='zh'>一个将元提示、上下文工程与基于规范的开发流程结合起来的框架，用于引导软件开发。通过标准化提示、强化工作流、提升团队协作的一致性与可重复性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://stevekrouse.com/precision" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Reports of code&#x27;s death are greatly exaggerated</span><span class='zh'>关于代码死亡的传闻被大大夸大</span></a><span class="dom">stevekrouse.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Code isn&#x27;t dead; AI tools will augment but not replace human software craftsmen.</span><span class='zh'>尽管有 AI 辅助写码的趋势，代码并未消亡。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Code isn&#x27;t dead; AI tools will augment but not replace human software craftsmen. The piece argues that precise, maintainable code, rigorous testing, and clear design remain essential as automation shifts routine work. In practice, expect a continued emphasis on tooling that preserves correctness and developer control.</span><span class='zh'>尽管有 AI 辅助写码的趋势，代码并未消亡。高质量、可维护的代码、严谨的测试和明确的设计仍然是关键，因为自动化会改变工作方式但需要人类把控。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.better-simple.com/django/2026/03/16/give-django-your-time-and-money/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Give Django your time and money, not your tokens</span><span class='zh'>给 Django 投入时间和金钱，而不是代币</span></a><span class="dom">better-simple.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Contribution to Django should come from real-world support—patches, code reviews, and sponsorship—rather than token-based schemes.</span><span class='zh'>对 Django 的长期发展，作者主张通过实际贡献来支撑，包括修补、代码评审和赞助，而不是代币化的资助方式。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Contribution to Django should come from real-world support—patches, code reviews, and sponsorship—rather than token-based schemes. Tokenized funding introduces volatility and governance risk that undermines a long-lived open source project. Real sponsorship and hands-on effort remain the reliable backbone.</span><span class='zh'>对 Django 的长期发展，作者主张通过实际贡献来支撑，包括修补、代码评审和赞助，而不是代币化的资助方式。代币化融资容易带来波动和治理风险，对这个重要的开源项目并不友好。真正可靠的支撑来自实际的赞助与人力投入。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gitlab.com/IsolatedOctopi/nvidia_greenboost" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nvidia greenboost: transparently extend GPU VRAM using system RAM/NVMe</span><span class='zh'>NVIDIA GreenBoost：透明扩展显存，利用系统内存/ NVMe</span></a><span class="dom">gitlab.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GreenBoost proposes using host RAM and NVMe as a transparent extension of GPU VRAM, enabling larger-than-GPU memory workloads with potential performance costs.</span><span class='zh'>GreenBoost 提议将主机内存与 NVMe 作为显存的透明扩展，使在显存受限时也能处理更大规模的工作负载，代价是潜在的性能开销和延迟。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GreenBoost proposes using host RAM and NVMe as a transparent extension of GPU VRAM, enabling larger-than-GPU memory workloads with potential performance costs. The project highlights memory management tradeoffs that could influence future GPU design.</span><span class='zh'>GreenBoost 提议将主机内存与 NVMe 作为显存的透明扩展，使在显存受限时也能处理更大规模的工作负载，代价是潜在的性能开销和延迟。该思路若落地，可能影响未来 GPU 的内存管理策略和驱动设计。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A $96 3D-printed rocket uses a $5 sensor to recalculate its trajectory mid-flight, showcasing how affordable components enable advanced control experiments.</span><span class='zh'>一个价值 96 美元的 3D 打印火箭，利用 5 美元传感器在飞行中重新计算轨迹，展示了低成本部件如何支持高端控制实验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://code.claude.com/docs/en/channels" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Push events into a running session with channels</span><span class='zh'>通过通道将事件推送到正在运行的会话</span></a><span class="dom">code.claude.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Push events into an active session using channels, enabling asynchronous event streams to feed a running process.</span><span class='zh'>通过通道将事件推送到正在运行的会话，使异步事件流可以实时进入一个正在执行的进程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Push events into an active session using channels, enabling asynchronous event streams to feed a running process. This pattern is useful for building interactive tools, REPLs, or simulations that need live data without blocking the main thread.</span><span class='zh'>通过通道将事件推送到正在运行的会话，使异步事件流可以实时进入一个正在执行的进程。该模式便于构建需要实时数据的交互式工具、REPL 或仿真，避免阻塞主线程。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://pbxscience.com/ubuntu-26-04-ends-46-years-of-silent-sudo-passwords/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ubuntu 26.04 Ends 46 Years of Silent sudo Passwords</span><span class='zh'>Ubuntu 26.04 结束46年来的静默 sudo 密码</span></a><span class="dom">pbxscience.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ubuntu 26.04 changes the default by requiring sudo passwords, ending decades of silent elevation.</span><span class='zh'>Ubuntu 26.04 将默认 sudo 提权改为需要密码，结束长久以来的静默便利。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ubuntu 26.04 changes the default by requiring sudo passwords, ending decades of silent elevation. The change boosts security hygiene but may affect onboarding and scripts; users can still fine-tune by policy.</span><span class='zh'>Ubuntu 26.04 将默认 sudo 提权改为需要密码，结束长久以来的静默便利。这一改动提升了系统安全，但可能增加开发和运维的耗时与脚本兼容性的问题，用户仍可通过策略进行调整。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/NVIDIA/NemoClaw" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nvidia NemoClaw</span><span class='zh'>NVIDIA 发布 NemoClaw</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>NVIDIA releases NemoClaw on GitHub as part of the NeMo AI tooling ecosystem.</span><span class='zh'>NVIDIA 在 GitHub 上推出 NemoClaw，作为 NeMo 人工智能工具生态的一部分。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>NVIDIA releases NemoClaw on GitHub as part of the NeMo AI tooling ecosystem. The project appears to be an experimental utility designed to simplify building or managing AI models, hinting at Nvidia&#x27;s ongoing push to modularize developer tooling for AI workloads. As with many in-progress projects, it signals where Nvidia is investing next in developer UX around AI.</span><span class='zh'>NVIDIA 在 GitHub 上推出 NemoClaw，作为 NeMo 人工智能工具生态的一部分。该项目似乎是一个实验性实用工具，旨在简化构建或管理 AI 模型，体现了 Nvidia 持续推动 AI 开发工具模块化的方向。对于这类尚在探索阶段的项目，它暗示了 Nvidia 在 AI 开发体验方面的下一步投资重点。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/danveloper/flash-moe" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Flash-MoE: Running a 397B Parameter Model on a Laptop</span><span class='zh'>Flash-MoE：在笔记本上运行397B参数模型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Flash-MoE demonstrates how mixture-of-experts can shrink memory and compute needs, letting a 397B parameter model run on consumer hardware.</span><span class='zh'>展示了混合专家（MoE）通过稀疏计算在普通硬件上运行大型模型的可能性，使397B参数模型在笔记本上落地成为现实。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Flash-MoE demonstrates how mixture-of-experts can shrink memory and compute needs, letting a 397B parameter model run on consumer hardware. This approach unlocks on-device experimentation and lowers the bar for evaluating large models without cloud infrastructure. Expect more on-device AI prototyping and edge-friendly deployments.</span><span class='zh'>展示了混合专家（MoE）通过稀疏计算在普通硬件上运行大型模型的可能性，使397B参数模型在笔记本上落地成为现实。此举降低了评估大模型对云资源的依赖，促进本地化实验与边缘部署的发展。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://huggingface.co/datasets/open-index/hacker-news" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Hacker News archive (47M+ items, 11.6GB) as Parquet, updated every 5m</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：以 Parquet 存储的 Hacker News 归档（4700万+ 条，11.6GB），每5分钟更新</span></a><span class="dom">huggingface.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An open dataset project publishing a massive Hacker News archive in Parquet format, enabling fast analytics and ML workflows.</span><span class='zh'>该开源数据集项目将 Hacker News 的庞大归档以 Parquet 格式发布，便于快速分析与机器学习工作流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An open dataset project publishing a massive Hacker News archive in Parquet format, enabling fast analytics and ML workflows. Updated every five minutes, it offers near-real-time access to historical data for researchers and enthusiasts. It&#x27;s a practical data infrastructure example for the HN ecosystem.</span><span class='zh'>该开源数据集项目将 Hacker News 的庞大归档以 Parquet 格式发布，便于快速分析与机器学习工作流。每五分钟更新一次，接近实时地提供历史数据，适合研究者与爱好者使用。这是面向 HN 生态的数据基础设施的一个实际案例。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://tooscut.app/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Professional video editing, right in the browser with WebGPU and WASM</span><span class='zh'>在浏览器中进行专业级视频编辑：WebGPU 与 WASM 力作</span></a><span class="dom">tooscut.app</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A browser-based video editor leverages WebGPU and WASM to deliver near-native professional editing capabilities.</span><span class='zh'>基于浏览器的视频编辑器利用 WebGPU 与 WASM 提供接近原生的专业编辑能力，实现快速迭代与隐私保护，但在某些方面仍可能落后于原生应用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A browser-based video editor leverages WebGPU and WASM to deliver near-native professional editing capabilities. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://notes.visaint.space/ai-coding-is-gambling/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI coding is gambling</span><span class='zh'>AI 编码像赌博一样</span></a><span class="dom">notes.visaint.space</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI-powered coding can introduce non-deterministic results and subtle bugs that escape tests.</span><span class='zh'>AI 辅助编程可能带来非确定性结果和隐藏缺陷，难以通过常规测试完全覆盖。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI-powered coding can introduce non-deterministic results and subtle bugs that escape tests. The piece argues that disciplined workflows, thorough reviews, and strong testing are essential when using code-generating AI. The takeaway: AI should augment developers, not replace careful craftsmanship.</span><span class='zh'>AI 辅助编程可能带来非确定性结果和隐藏缺陷，难以通过常规测试完全覆盖。文章强调在使用代码生成型 AI 时，需建立严格的工作流、充分的代码审查与测试来降低风险。要点是把 AI 作为辅助工具，而不是替代高质量的编码实践。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://tomjohnell.com/llms-can-be-absolutely-exhausting/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>LLMs can be exhausting</span><span class='zh'>大模型让人感到精疲力竭</span></a><span class="dom">tomjohnell.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Interacting with large language models can be exhausting for users due to cognitive load, iterative prompting, and context management.</span><span class='zh'>与大模型交互由于认知负荷、不断迭代的提示及上下文管理，可能让人感到精疲力竭。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Interacting with large language models can be exhausting for users due to cognitive load, iterative prompting, and context management. The piece argues for better tooling and robust workflows to prevent fatigue and keep productivity high.</span><span class='zh'>与大模型交互由于认知负荷、不断迭代的提示及上下文管理，可能让人感到精疲力竭。文章主张改进工具与工作流程，降低疲劳并保持生产力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://isaacfreund.com/blog/river-window-management/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Separating the Wayland compositor and window manager</span><span class='zh'>将 Wayland 合成器与窗口管理器分离</span></a><span class="dom">isaacfreund.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>River and similar designs separate Wayland&#x27;s compositor from window-management logic, enabling more modular, testable components.</span><span class='zh'>Wayland 合成器与窗口管理器的分离理念，推动组件化、可测试性提升。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>River and similar designs separate Wayland&#x27;s compositor from window-management logic, enabling more modular, testable components. The post argues this separation can improve security, maintainability, and experimentation in tiling and stacking behaviors.</span><span class='zh'>Wayland 合成器与窗口管理器的分离理念，推动组件化、可测试性提升。文章认为这种分离能提升安全性、可维护性，并促进在平铺式和叠加式窗口管理上的实验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://andrewmurphy.io/blog/if-you-thought-the-speed-of-writing-code-was-your-problem-you-have-bigger-problems" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>If you thought code writing speed was your problem you have bigger problems</span><span class='zh'>以为写代码速度是问题？你还有更大的问题</span></a><span class="dom">andrewmurphy.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Code-writing speed isn’t the main bottleneck; architecture, design, and maintainability are.</span><span class='zh'>写代码速度并非问题的核心，架构设计与可维护性才是关键。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Code-writing speed isn’t the main bottleneck; architecture, design, and maintainability are. Focusing on speed without addressing these systemic challenges yields brittle code and higher long-term toil.</span><span class='zh'>写代码速度并非问题的核心，架构设计与可维护性才是关键。若只追求速度而忽略系统层面的挑战，最终会产生脆弱的实现和额外的后续工作。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/cockpit-project/cockpit" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cockpit is a web-based graphical interface for servers</span><span class='zh'>Cockpit：一个基于网页的服务器图形界面</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cockpit provides a browser-based UI to monitor and manage Linux servers, integrating with system services, containers, and logs to simplify remote administration beyond SSH-only workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Cockpit 提供基于浏览器的服务器管理界面，便于远程监控和配置 Linux 服务器，覆盖系统服务、容器与日志等功能，降低了对 SSH 作为唯一入口的依赖。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cockpit provides a browser-based UI to monitor and manage Linux servers, integrating with system services, containers, and logs to simplify remote administration beyond SSH-only workflows. Its modular, open-source approach makes it appealing for ops teams seeking a lightweight control plane.</span><span class='zh'>Cockpit 提供基于浏览器的服务器管理界面，便于远程监控和配置 Linux 服务器，覆盖系统服务、容器与日志等功能，降低了对 SSH 作为唯一入口的依赖。其模块化、开源特性使运维团队更容易搭建轻量级的控制平面。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Show HN demonstrates duplicating three layers inside a 24B LLM to improve logical deduction by about 0.22–0.76 without any training.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 展示了在一个 24B 参数的语言模型中复制三层的做法，未进行任何再训练就实现了逻辑推理能力的提升（约 0.22–0.76）。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Show HN demonstrates duplicating three layers inside a 24B LLM to improve logical deduction by about 0.22–0.76 without any training. The work illustrates how targeted architectural tweaks can boost reasoning without changing data or parameters, highlighting ongoing exploration of model-internal mechanisms for reasoning.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 展示了在一个 24B 参数的语言模型中复制三层的做法，未进行任何再训练就实现了逻辑推理能力的提升（约 0.22–0.76）。该工作突显通过内部网络结构调整来增强推理能力的潜力，推动对模型内部机制的探索。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/pyodide/pyodide" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Pyodide: a Python distribution based on WebAssembly</span><span class='zh'>Pyodide：基于 WebAssembly 的 Python 发行版</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Pyodide brings Python to the browser via WebAssembly, enabling data science stacks like NumPy, Pandas to run in the client.</span><span class='zh'>Pyodide 将 Python 通过 WebAssembly 运行在浏览器中，使前端直接运行 NumPy、Pandas 等数据科学工具。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Pyodide brings Python to the browser via WebAssembly, enabling data science stacks like NumPy, Pandas to run in the client. It enables interactive notebooks and offline data experiments, lowering the barrier to high-level Python on the web.</span><span class='zh'>Pyodide 将 Python 通过 WebAssembly 运行在浏览器中，使前端直接运行 NumPy、Pandas 等数据科学工具。它为离线使用、教育示例和轻量级数据分析提供了新的前端能力，改变了在浏览器端进行 Python 开发的方式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/RasKrebs/sonar" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Sonar – A tiny CLI to see and kill whatever&#x27;s running on localhost</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Sonar — 一个查看并终止本地运行服务的小巧 CLI</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Sonar is a tiny command‑line utility that lists what&#x27;s listening on localhost and lets you kill offending processes quickly, speeding up developer workflows when ports are occupied.</span><span class='zh'>Sonar 是一个小巧的命令行工具，可列出本地监听端口并快速终止占用进程，方便在端口被占用时加速开发流程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Sonar is a tiny command‑line utility that lists what&#x27;s listening on localhost and lets you kill offending processes quickly, speeding up developer workflows when ports are occupied. It&#x27;s a pragmatic, cross‑platform tool for reclaiming ports without digging through process managers or GUI tools.</span><span class='zh'>Sonar 是一个小巧的命令行工具，可列出本地监听端口并快速终止占用进程，方便在端口被占用时加速开发流程。它以实用为主，跨平台地帮助开发者无需翻找复杂进程管理器即可回收端口。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mr-fatalyst/oxyde" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Oxyde – Pydantic-native async ORM with a Rust core</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Oxyde——基于 Pydantic 的原生异步 ORM，核心为 Rust</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Oxyde is a Python ORM that uses Pydantic for data validation and typing, with a Rust core delivering performance and safety.</span><span class='zh'>Oxyde 是一个 Python ORM，基于 Pydantic 进行数据校验和类型定义，核心用 Rust 实现以提升性能和安全性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Oxyde is a Python ORM that uses Pydantic for data validation and typing, with a Rust core delivering performance and safety. It aims to offer a modern, async-first ORM experience that competes with SQLAlchemy-style tooling, potentially changing how Python apps model and query data.</span><span class='zh'>Oxyde 是一个 Python ORM，基于 Pydantic 进行数据校验和类型定义，核心用 Rust 实现以提升性能和安全性。它强调异步优先的使用体验，可能对现有的 SQLAlchemy 等工具形成竞争，并改变 Python 应用的数据建模与查询方式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/creationix/rx" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>RX – a new random-access JSON alternative</span><span class='zh'>RX：一种新的随机访问 JSON 替代方案</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>RX offers a data format with random-access capabilities that aims to outperform JSON on certain workloads, enabling direct access to nested structures without full parsing.</span><span class='zh'>RX 提供具备随机访问能力的新的数据格式，旨在在某些工作负载上优于 JSON，实现对嵌套结构的直接访问而无需完整解析。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>RX offers a data format with random-access capabilities that aims to outperform JSON on certain workloads, enabling direct access to nested structures without full parsing. It uses a binary layout with indexing and streaming semantics, suited for data intensive apps.</span><span class='zh'>RX 提供具备随机访问能力的新的数据格式，旨在在某些工作负载上优于 JSON，实现对嵌套结构的直接访问而无需完整解析。它采用二进制格式并带有索引与流式语义，适用于数据密集型应用，但需配套的工具链和设计模式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/aquasecurity/trivy/security/advisories/GHSA-69fq-xp46-6x23" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Trivy ecosystem supply chain briefly compromised</span><span class='zh'>Trivy 生态系统供应链短暂受损</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A security advisory reports a brief compromise in the Trivy ecosystem, prompting updates and coordinated responses for users to mitigate supply-chain risk.</span><span class='zh'>Trivy 生态系统短暂受损的安全公告促使用户更新并采取应对措施，以降低供应链风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A security advisory reports a brief compromise in the Trivy ecosystem, prompting updates and coordinated responses for users to mitigate supply-chain risk. The incident underscores the fragility of open-source tooling and the need for rapid patching.</span><span class='zh'>Trivy 生态系统短暂受损的安全公告促使用户更新并采取应对措施，以降低供应链风险。事件凸显开源工具的脆弱性及快速修补的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/robotocore/robotocore" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Robotocore · a Digital Twin of AWS</span><span class='zh'>Robotocore：AWS 的数字孪生</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Robotocore provides a digital twin of AWS, enabling offline testing and simulation of cloud workloads.</span><span class='zh'>Robotocore 提供了 AWS 的数字孪生，便于在本地离线仿真云工作负载、测试基础设施变更，降低成本与风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Robotocore provides a digital twin of AWS, enabling offline testing and simulation of cloud workloads. By modeling cloud infrastructure as code, it helps operators prototype changes with reduced risk and cost.</span><span class='zh'>Robotocore 提供了 AWS 的数字孪生，便于在本地离线仿真云工作负载、测试基础设施变更，降低成本与风险。该开源项目推动云原生环境的本地化开发与试验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/embesozzi/keycloak-openfga-event-publisher" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Event Publisher enables event integration between Keycloak and OpenFGA</span><span class='zh'>Event Publisher 让 Keycloak 与 OpenFGA 之间实现事件驱动的集成</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Event Publisher enables event-driven integration between Keycloak and OpenFGA, syncing identity and authorization decisions in real time.</span><span class='zh'>Event Publisher 让 Keycloak 与 OpenFGA 之间实现事件驱动的集成，实时同步身份与授权决策，帮助跨应用执行策略驱动的访问控制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Event Publisher enables event-driven integration between Keycloak and OpenFGA, syncing identity and authorization decisions in real time. This helps enforce policy-driven access across apps.</span><span class='zh'>Event Publisher 让 Keycloak 与 OpenFGA 之间实现事件驱动的集成，实时同步身份与授权决策，帮助跨应用执行策略驱动的访问控制。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/YuminosukeSato/ironkernel" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>How HN: Ironkernel – Python expressions, Rust parallel</span><span class='zh'>Ironkernel：Python 表达式、Rust 并行</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ironkernel is a project combining Python-style expressions with Rust-backed parallel execution to speed up evaluation tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Ironkernel 项目将 Python 风格的表达式与 Rust 的并行执行结合，提升表达式计算的性能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ironkernel is a project combining Python-style expressions with Rust-backed parallel execution to speed up evaluation tasks. It explores language design and performance trade-offs between dynamic expression evaluation and compiled performance.</span><span class='zh'>Ironkernel 项目将 Python 风格的表达式与 Rust 的并行执行结合，提升表达式计算的性能。它讨论了动态表达式语言与编译型性能之间的权衡以及实现细节。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/jeffrey-sardina/systemd" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Liberated Systemd</span><span class='zh'>Liberated Systemd</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A project aiming to liberate systemd by loosening its control or creating alternatives; discusses the motivations, potential benefits, and trade-offs of decoupling init systems.</span><span class='zh'>一个旨在解放或替代 Systemd 的项目，讨论去中心化、降低对单一初始化系统的依赖的动机、潜在收益及权衡。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A project aiming to liberate systemd by loosening its control or creating alternatives; discusses the motivations, potential benefits, and trade-offs of decoupling init systems.</span><span class='zh'>一个旨在解放或替代 Systemd 的项目，讨论去中心化、降低对单一初始化系统的依赖的动机、潜在收益及权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/QAInsights/AIx" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Quillx is an open standard for disclosing AI involvement in software projects</span><span class='zh'>Quillx 开放标准：披露软件项目中的 AI 参与</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Presents Quillx, an open standard for disclosing AI involvement in software projects, enabling transparency about machine-generated contributions and AI-assisted components.</span><span class='zh'>介绍 Quillx，一项用于披露软件项目中 AI 参与的开放标准，旨在提升对机器生成贡献和 AI 辅助组件的透明度。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Presents Quillx, an open standard for disclosing AI involvement in software projects, enabling transparency about machine-generated contributions and AI-assisted components. The aim is to foster trust, accountability, and clearer licensing in human-AI workflows.</span><span class='zh'>介绍 Quillx，一项用于披露软件项目中 AI 参与的开放标准，旨在提升对机器生成贡献和 AI 辅助组件的透明度。助力建立信任、问责以及更清晰的混合人机工作流程的许可边界。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rhodey/hecate" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Hecate – Call an AI from Signal</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Hecate — 通过 Signal 调用 AI</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Hecate lets users call an AI from within Signal, opening new pathways for private AI interactions within messaging apps.</span><span class='zh'>Hecate 允许在 Signal 中直接调用 AI，开启在端对端加密的聊天环境中对 AI 的新访问路径。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Hecate lets users call an AI from within Signal, opening new pathways for private AI interactions within messaging apps. The project demonstrates how AI backends can be accessed from end-to-end encrypted chats, raising questions about privacy, latency, and bot capabilities.</span><span class='zh'>Hecate 允许在 Signal 中直接调用 AI，开启在端对端加密的聊天环境中对 AI 的新访问路径。该项目展示了将 AI 后端接入即时通讯应用的可行性，同时也引发关于隐私、时延和机器人能力的讨论。</span></p>
